```text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""
3	Exp-A: Non-differential Misclassification Stress Test Expansion
4	
5	Tests sign flip rate and bias ratio under increasing differential
6	misclassification departure ε.
7	
8	4 topologies × 2 K × 3 CM qualities × 4 ε = 96 configs
9	N=10,000 × 100 MC reps per config
10	"""
11	import numpy as np
12	import json
13	import time
14	import os
15	
16	TOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'mediation', 'exposure', 'iv']
17	K_VALUES = [3, 10]
18	EPSILONS = [0.0, 0.01, 0.05, 0.10]
19	CM_QUALS = {'high': 0.8, 'medium': 0.6, 'low': 0.4}
20	N_SAMPLES = 10_000
21	N_REPS = 100
22	N_LARGE = 2_000_000
23	SEED = 42
24	
25	_ALPHA_POOL = [1.5, -1.0, 0.8, -0.5, 1.2, -0.7, 0.9, -0.4, 0.6]
26	_BETA_A_POOL = [1.0, -0.5, 0.7, -0.3, 0.5, -0.2, 0.4, -0.15, 0.3]
27	_BETA_POOL = [0.0, 1.0, -0.5, 0.7, -0.3, 0.5, -0.2, 0.4, -0.15, 0.3]
28	_GAMMA_POOL = [0.0, 0.5, -0.3, 0.4, -0.2, 0.3, -0.1, 0.2, -0.05, 0.1]
29	_DELTA_POOL = [0.0, 0.8, -0.6, 0.5, -0.4, 0.3, -0.2, 0.15, -0.1, 0.05]
30	
31	
32	def softmax_rows(x):
33	    e = np.exp(x - x.max(axis=1, keepdims=True))
34	    return e / e.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
35	
36	
37	def sample_cat(probs, rng):
38	    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)
39	    u = rng.uniform(size=probs.shape[0])[:, None]
40	    return (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)
41	
42	
43	def make_p_A(K):
44	    if K == 3:
45	        return np.array([0.40, 0.35, 0.25])
46	    raw = np.array([1.0 / (i + 1) for i in range(K)])
47	    return raw / raw.sum()
48	
49	
50	def make_dgp_params(K):
51	    p_A = make_p_A(K)
52	    params = {
53	        'confounding': {
54	            'p_A': p_A, 'alpha': np.array(_ALPHA_POOL[:K-1]),
55	            'beta_A': np.array(_BETA_A_POOL[:K-1]),
56	            'beta_T': 2.0, 'sigma_T': 1.0,
57	        },
58	        'mediation': {
59	            'tau': 0.5, 'gamma': np.array(_GAMMA_POOL[:K]),
60	            'delta': np.array(_DELTA_POOL[:K]),
61	            'beta': np.array(_BETA_POOL[:K]),
62	        },
63	        'exposure': {'beta': np.array(_BETA_POOL[:K]), 'p_A': p_A},
64	    }
65	    if K == 3:
66	        params['iv'] = {
67	            'lam': 1.0, 'beta': np.array([0.0, 0.5, -0.25]),
68	            'g': np.zeros(3), 'dZ': np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5]),
69	            'dU': np.array([0.0, 0.8, 0.6]),
70	        }
71	    else:
72	        iv_b = np.array(_BETA_POOL[:K]) * 0.5
73	        iv_b[0] = 0.0
74	        params['iv'] = {
75	            'lam': 1.0, 'beta': iv_b, 'g': np.zeros(K),
76	            'dZ': np.array([0.0] + [_ALPHA_POOL[i]*0.7 for i in range(K-1)]),
77	            'dU': np.array([0.0] + [_BETA_A_POOL[i]*0.6 for i in range(K-1)]),
78	        }
79	    return params
80	
81	
82	# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────
83	
84	def gen_confounding(K, N, p, rng):
85	    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=p['p_A'])
86	    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))
87	    for j in range(K-1):
88	        D[:, j] = (A == j+1).astype(float)
89	    T = D @ p['alpha'] + rng.normal(0, p['sigma_T'], N)
90	    Y = p['beta_T']*T + D @ p['beta_A'] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)
91	    return T, Y, A
92	
93	
94	def gen_mediation(K, N, p, rng):
95	    T = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, N).astype(float)
96	    logits = p['gamma'][None, :] + p['delta'][None, :]*T[:, None]
97	    A = sample_cat(softmax_rows(logits), rng)
98	    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))
99	    for j in range(K-1):
100	        D[:, j] = (A == j+1).astype(float)
101	    Y = p['beta'][0] + D @ p['beta'][1:] + p['tau']*T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)
102	    return T, Y, A
103	
104	
105	def gen_exposure(K, N, p, rng):
106	    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=p['p_A'])
107	    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))
108	    for j in range(K-1):
109	        D[:, j] = (A == j+1).astype(float)
110	    Y = p['beta'][0] + D @ p['beta'][1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)
111	    return None, Y, A
112	
113	
114	def gen_iv(K, N, p, rng):
115	    Z = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, N).astype(float)
116	    U = rng.normal(0, 1, N)
117	    logits = (p['g'][None, :] + p['dZ'][None, :]*Z[:, None]
118	              + p['dU'][None, :]*U[:, None])
119	    A = sample_cat(softmax_rows(logits), rng)
120	    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))
121	    for j in range(K-1):
122	        D[:, j] = (A == j+1).astype(float)
123	    Y = D @ p['beta'][1:] + p['lam']*U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)
124	    return Z, Y, A
125	
126	
127	GEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'mediation': gen_mediation,
128	       'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}
129	
130	
131	# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────
132	
133	def est_tau(topo, fv, Y, Ak, K):
134	    N = len(Y)
135	    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))
136	    for k in range(1, K):
137	        D[:, k-1] = (Ak == k).astype(float)
138	    if topo == 'iv':
139	        z1, z0 = fv == 1, fv == 0
140	        if z1.sum() < 2 or z0.sum() < 2:
141	            return np.nan
142	        RF = Y[z1].mean() - Y[z0].mean()
143	        FS = D[z1, 0].mean() - D[z0, 0].mean()
144	        return RF / FS if abs(FS) > 1e-8 else np.nan
145	    elif topo == 'exposure':
146	        X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D])
147	    else:
148	        X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), fv, D])
149	    try:
150	        return np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0][1]
151	    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
152	        return np.nan
153	
154	
155	# ── Confusion matrix construction & perturbation ────────────
156	
157	def make_cm(K, diag):
158	    C = np.full((K, K), (1.0 - diag) / (K - 1))
159	    np.fill_diagonal(C, diag)
160	    return C
161	
162	
163	def perturb(C, eps):
164	    K = C.shape[0]
165	    Cp = C.copy()
166	    Cp[~np.eye(K, dtype=bool)] += eps
167	    for j in range(K):
168	        Cp[:, j] /= Cp[:, j].sum()
169	    return Cp
170	
171	
172	def gen_Astar(A, Y, Cb, eps, rng):
173	    if eps <= 0:
174	        probs = Cb[:, A]
175	    else:
176	        Cp = perturb(Cb, eps)
177	        high = Y > np.median(Y)
178	        probs = np.where(high[None, :], Cp[:, A], Cb[:, A])
179	    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)
180	    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))
181	    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)
182	
183	
184	# ── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
185	
186	def main():
187	    t0 = time.time()
188	    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
189	    n_total = len(TOPOLOGIES) * len(K_VALUES) * len(EPSILONS) * len(CM_QUALS)
190	    print(f"Exp-A: {n_total} configs x {N_REPS} MC reps, N={N_SAMPLES}")
191	
192	    # Validate CMs
193	    for K in K_VALUES:
194	        for dv in CM_QUALS.values():
195	            C = make_cm(K, dv)
196	            assert np.allclose(C.sum(axis=0), 1.0), f"K={K} diag={dv}: bad CM"
197	            for eps in EPSILONS:
198	                if eps > 0:
199	                    Cp = perturb(C, eps)
200	                    assert np.allclose(Cp.sum(axis=0), 1.0)
201	                    assert (Cp >= -1e-12).all()
202	
203	    all_params = {K: make_dgp_params(K) for K in K_VALUES}
204	
205	    # True parameters (oracle with N=2M)
206	    tau_trues = {}
207	    print("\nTrue parameters (N=2M oracle):")
208	    for K in K_VALUES:
209	        for topo in TOPOLOGIES:
210	            rng = np.random.default_rng(K*1000 + abs(hash(topo)) % 1000)
211	            fv, Y, A = GEN[topo](K, N_LARGE, all_params[K][topo], rng)
212	            tau_trues[(K, topo)] = est_tau(topo, fv, Y, A, K)
213	            print(f"  K={K:>2} {topo:<13} tau_true={tau_trues[(K,topo)]:>10.6f}")
214	
215	    # MC simulation
216	    results = []
217	    idx = 0
218	    print(f"\nMC simulation ({n_total} configs):")
219	    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:
220	        for K in K_VALUES:
221	            p = all_params[K][topo]
222	            tt = tau_trues[(K, topo)]
223	            for qn, dv in CM_QUALS.items():
224	                Cb = make_cm(K, dv)
225	                for eps in EPSILONS:
226	                    idx += 1
227	                    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + idx*1000)
228	                    hats = np.empty(N_REPS)
229	                    for m in range(N_REPS):
230	                        fv, Y, A = GEN[topo](K, N_SAMPLES, p, rng)
231	                        As = gen_Astar(A, Y, Cb, eps, rng)
232	                        hats[m] = est_tau(topo, fv, Y, As, K)
233	
234	                    ok = np.isfinite(hats)
235	                    nv = int(ok.sum())
236	                    if nv > 0 and abs(tt) > 1e-12:
237	                        vh = hats[ok]
238	                        sfr = float((np.sign(vh) != np.sign(tt)).mean())
239	                        ratios = np.abs(vh / tt)
240	                        br = float(ratios.mean())
241	                        br_std = float(ratios.std())
242	                        br_med = float(np.median(ratios))
243	                        mt = float(vh.mean())
244	                    else:
245	                        sfr = br = br_std = br_med = mt = None
246	
247	                    results.append({
248	                        'topology': topo, 'K': K, 'epsilon': eps,
249	                        'cm_quality': qn, 'cm_diag': dv,
250	                        'sign_flip_rate': sfr,
251	                        'mean_bias_ratio': br,
252	                        'std_bias_ratio': br_std,
253	                        'median_bias_ratio': br_med,
254	                        'tau_true': float(tt),
255	                        'mean_tau_hat': mt,
256	                        'n_reps': nv,
257	                    })
258	
259	                    if idx % 12 == 0 or idx == n_total:
260	                        el = time.time() - t0
261	                        eta = el / idx * (n_total - idx)
262	                        s = f"SF={sfr:.3f} BR={br:.3f}" if sfr is not None else "N/A"
263	                        print(f"  [{idx:>3}/{n_total}] {topo:<13} K={K:>2} "
264	                              f"eps={eps:.2f} {qn:<6} {s}  "
265	                              f"({el:.0f}s, ETA {eta:.0f}s)")
266	
267	    # Summaries
268	    def sm(vals):
269	        v = [x for x in vals if x is not None]
270	        return float(np.mean(v)) if v else None
271	
272	    by_eps = {}
273	    for eps in EPSILONS:
274	        sub = [r for r in results if r['epsilon'] == eps]
275	        sfs = [r['sign_flip_rate'] for r in sub if r['sign_flip_rate'] is not None]
276	        brs = [r['mean_bias_ratio'] for r in sub if r['mean_bias_ratio'] is not None]
277	        by_eps[str(eps)] = {
278	            'mean_sign_flip_rate': float(np.mean(sfs)) if sfs else None,
279	            'mean_bias_ratio': float(np.mean(brs)) if brs else None,
280	            'max_sign_flip_rate': float(max(sfs)) if sfs else None,
281	        }
282	
283	    by_topo = {}
284	    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:
285	        sub = [r for r in results if r['topology'] == topo]
286	        sfs = [r['sign_flip_rate'] for r in sub if r['sign_flip_rate'] is not None]
287	        brs = [r['mean_bias_ratio'] for r in sub if r['mean_bias_ratio'] is not None]
288	        by_topo[topo] = {
289	            'mean_sign_flip_rate': float(np.mean(sfs)) if sfs else None,
290	            'mean_bias_ratio': float(np.mean(brs)) if brs else None,
291	        }
292	
293	    by_K = {}
294	    for K in K_VALUES:

297	        brs = [r['mean_bias_ratio'] for r in sub if r['mean_bias_ratio'] is not None]
298	        by_K[str(K)] = {

301	        }

304	        'results': results,

308	    def clean(o):
309	        if isinstance(o, (float, np.floating)):
310	            v = float(o)
311	            return None if (np.isnan(v) or np.isinf(v)) else v
312	        if isinstance(o, np.integer):
313	            return int(o)
314	        if isinstance(o, dict):
315	            return {k: clean(v) for k, v in o.items()}
316	        if isinstance(o, list):
317	            return [clean(v) for v in o]
318	        return o
319

348	def write_tex(results, path):

351	        r'\begin{table*}[t]',

u = rng.uniform(size=probs.shape[0])[:, None]\n    return (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n\n\ndef make_p_A(K):\n    if K == 3:\n        return np.array([0.40, 0.35, 0.25])\n    raw = np.array([1.0 / (i + 1) for i in range(K)])\n    return raw / raw.sum()\n\n\ndef make_dgp_params(K):\n    p_A = make_p_A(K)\n    params = {\n        'confounding': {\n            'p_A': p_A, 'alpha': range(K-1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j+1).astype(float)\n    T = D @ p['alpha'] + rng.normal(0, p['sigma_T'], N)\n    Y = p['beta_T']*T + D @ p['beta_A'] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    return T, Y, A\n\n\ndef gen_mediation(K, N, p, rng):\n    T = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, N).astype(float)\n    logits = p['gamma'][None, :] + p['delta'][None, :]*T[:, None]\n    A = sample_cat(softmax_rows(logits), rng)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))\n    for j in range(K-1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j+1).astype(float)\n    Y = p['beta'][0] + D @ p['beta'][1:] + p['tau']*T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    return T, Y, p['beta'][0] + D @ p['beta'][1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    return None, Y, A\n\n\ndef gen_iv(K, N, p, rng):\n    Z = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, N).astype(float)\n    U = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_tau(topo, fv, Y, Ak, K):\n    N = len(Y)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K-1))\n    for k   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nExp-B: Logistic DGP Simulation\n\nValidates sign-flip findings under nonlinear (logistic regression) DGP.\n3 topologies (confounding, exposure, IV) × 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)[:, None]\n    A = (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    eta = D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef compute_true_params():\n    N_oracle = 2_000_000\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(9999)\n    true = {}\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_confounding(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    true['confounding'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_exposure(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\n    true['exposure'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, D_true = gen_iv(N_oracle, rng)\n    coef_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_true)\n    coef_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_true)\n    true['iv'] = {\n        'linear_2sls': coef_2sls.tolist(),\n        'control_function': coef_cf.tolist(),\n    }\n    return true\n\n\n# ── MC simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_mc(cms):\n    true_params = compute_true_params()\n    print(\"True parameters (oracle N=2M):\")\n    for topo, vals in true_params.items():\n        for model, coefs in vals.items():\n            print(f\"  {topo}/{model}: {[f'{c:.4f}' for c in coefs]}\")\n\n    results = []\n    n_configs = len(TOPOLOGIES) * len(cms)\n    idx = 0\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        for cm_name, C in cms.items():\n            idx += 1\n            rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + idx * 1000)\n            short_name = cm_name.replace('vast/', '')\n\n            if topo == 'confounding':\n                true_log = np.array(true_params['confounding']['logistic'])\n                true_lin = np.array(true_params['confounding']['linear'])\n            elif topo == 'exposure':\n                true_log = np.array(true_params['exposure']['logistic'])\n                true_lin = np.array(true_params['exposure']['linear'])\n            else:\n                true_2sls = np.array(true_params['iv']['linear_2sls'])\n                true_cf = np.array(true_params['iv']['control_function'])\n\n            log_coefs = []\n            lin_coefs = []\n            log_sf = 0\n            lin_sf = 0\n            valid = 0\n\n            for m in range(N_REPS):\n                T, Y, A, D_true = GEN[topo](N_SAMPLES, rng)\n                A_star = misclassify(A, C, rng)\n                D_star = dummy_encode(A_star)\n\n                if topo np.all(np.isfinite(c_log)) and np.all(np.isfinite(c_lin)):\n                        valid += 1\n                        log_coefs.append(c_log)\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_lin)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)):\n                            log_sf += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin) != np.sign(true_lin)):\n                            lin_sf += 1\n\n                elif topo == 'exposure':\n                    c_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star)\n                    c_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star)\n                    if np.all(np.isfinite(c_log)) and np.all(np.isfinite(c_lin)):\n                        valid += 1\n                        log_coefs.append(c_log)\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_lin)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)):\n                            log_sf += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin) != np.sign(true_lin)):\n                            lin_sf += 1\n\n                elif topo == 'iv':\n                    c_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star)\n                    c_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star)\n                    if np.all(np.isfinite(c_2sls)) and np.all(np.isfinite(c_cf)):\n                        valid += 1\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_2sls)\n                        log_coefs.append(c_cf)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_2sls) != np.sign(true_2sls)):\n                            lin_sf += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_cf) != np.sign(true_cf)):\n                            log_sf += 1\n\n            if valid > 0:\n                log_arr = np.array(log_coefs)\n                lin_arr f\"valid={valid}/{N_REPS} ({el:.0f}s, ETA {eta:.0f}s)\")\n\n    return results, true_params\n\n\ndef build_comparison(results):\n    comp = {}\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        sub = [r for r in 'n_cms': len(log_sfs),\n        }\n    return comp\n\n\ndef write_tex(results, path):\n    lines = [\n        r'\\begin{table}[t]',\n        r'\\centering',\n        r'\\caption{Sign flip rates: v = float(o)\n        return None if (np.isnan(v) or np.isinf(v)) else v\n    if isinstance(o, np.integer):\n        return int(o)\n    if isinstance(o, dict):\n        return {k: clean(v) for k, v in o.items()}\n    if isinstance(o, list):\n        return [clean(v) for v in o]\n    return o\n\n\ndef main():\n    t0 = time.time()\n    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n    cm_path = os.path.join(out_dir, 'plan011_frontier_cms.json')\n\n    cms = load_vast_cms(cm_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(cms)} VAST K=3 CMs: {list(cms.keys())}\")\n\n    results, true_params = print(\"\\nComparison summary:\")\n    for topo, c in comp.items():\n        nl = c['nonlinear_mean_sf']\n        li = c['linear_mean_sf']\n        nls = f\"{nl:.4f}\" if nl is not None else 'N/A'\n     "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nExp-B: Logistic DGP Simulation\n\nValidates sign-flip findings under nonlinear (logistic regression) DGP.\n3 topologies (confounding, exposure, IV) × 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)[:, None]\n    A = (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    eta = D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef   T, Y, A, _ = gen_exposure(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\n    true['exposure'] = {\n        'logistic': {
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, 'artifacts')\n# Quick smoke test with reduced params\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Patch before import\nimport exp_b_logistic_dgp as eb\neb.N_SAMPLES = 1000\neb.N_REPS = 5\n\ncms = eb.load_vast_cms('artifacts/plan011_frontier_cms.json')\nprint(f'CMs loaded: {len(cms)}')\nfor name, cm in cms.items():\n    print(f'  {name}: shape={cm.shape}, col_sums={cm.sum(axis=0)}')\n\n# Test each DGP\nrng = np.random.default_rng(42)\nfor topo in eb.TOPOLOGIES:\n    T, Y, A, extra = eb.GEN[topo](1000, rng)\n    D = eb.dummy_encode(A)\n    print(f'{topo}: T_shape={T.shape if T is not None else None}, Y_mean={Y.mean():.3f}, A_unique={np.unique(A)}, D_shape={D.shape}')\n\n# Test estimators\nT, Y, A, _ = eb.gen_confounding(1000, rng)\nD = eb.dummy_encode(A)\nc_log = eb.est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D)\nc_lin = eb.est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D)\nprint(f'Confounding logistic coefs: {c_log}')\nprint(f'Confounding linear coefs: {c_lin}')\n\nT, Y, A, _ = eb.gen_exposure(1000, rng)\nD = eb.dummy_encode(A)\nc_log = eb.est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\nc_lin = eb.est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\nprint(f'Exposure logistic coefs: {c_log}')\nprint(f'Exposure linear coefs: {c_lin}')\n\nT, Y, A, D_true = eb.gen_iv(1000, rng)\nc_2sls = eb.est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_true)\nc_cf = eb.est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_true)\nprint(f'IV 2SLS coefs: {c_2sls}')\nprint(f'IV control function coefs: {c_cf}')\n\n# Test misclassify\nC = list(cms.values())[0]\nA_star = eb.misclassify(A, C, rng)\nprint(f'Misclassify: unique A*={np.unique(A_star)}')\nprint('Smoke test passed.')\n\"\n",
  "description": "Smoke test the Exp-B script with reduced params",
  "timeout": 60000
}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Good, the smoke test passes. Let me fix the sklearn deprecation warning by changing `penalty=None` to `C=np.inf`.

Also, I notice that the logistic and linear coefficients have the expected sign patterns: BETA = [0, 1.0, -0.5], so the exposure coefficients should be positive for D1 and negative for D2. The logistic gives [0.9, -0.3] and the linear gives [0.21, -0.07] — both consistent in sign.

For confounding, the logistic gives [τ, β1, β2] = [0.40, 0.79, -0.42] which matches the expected signs.


Now I'll run a small Monte Carlo simulation to verify that the sign flips behave as expected when I flip the outcome or exposure variables.
```

× 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(penalty=None, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef In the confounding case where A→T and A→Y, when A gets misclassified to A*, the coefficients on the mislabeled variables can be attenuated, but τ's sign should be preserved because the confounding structure creates bias through omitted variable bias, not sign reversal. The random sign flips I'm seeing with only 20 reps and small N are just noise — I need to look at the true confounding coefficients more carefully to understand what's actually happening with the small τ value.

The real issue is that the linear probability model's τ is only around 0.093, which is tiny compared to the logistic model's τ=0.5 due to sigmoid compression. With misclassified dummies, this small coefficient could easily flip signs from noise alone. But the specification is actually about the paper's theoretical framework, which predicts sign flips for the mislabeled variable coefficients (A/D), not necessarily the treatment effect. The validation criteria distinguish between exposure topology (which should show sign flips) and confounding topology (which shouldn't), and I need to check what happens when comparing linear versus logistic models.

146	    N = len(Y)

      "content": "        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef compute_true_params():\n    N_oracle = 2_000_000\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(9999)\n    true = {}\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_confounding(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    true['confounding'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_exposure(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\n    true['exposure'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, D_true = gen_iv(N_oracle, rng)\n    coef_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_true)\n    coef_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_true)\n    true['iv'] = {\n        'linear_2sls': coef_2sls.tolist(),\n        'control_function': coef_cf.tolist(),\n    }\n    return true\n\n\n# ── MC simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_mc(cms):\n    true_params = compute_true_params()\n    print(\"True parameters (oracle N=2M):\")\n    for topo, vals in true_params.items():\n        for model, coefs in vals.items():\n            print(f\"  {topo}/{model}: {[f'{c:.4f}' for c in coefs]}\")\n\n    results = []\n    n_configs = len(TOPOLOGIES) * len(cms)\n    idx = 0\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        for cm_name, C in cms.items():",

    "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nExp-B: Logistic DGP Simulation\n\nValidates sign-flip findings under nonlinear (logistic regression) DGP.\n3 topologies (confounding, exposure, IV) × 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)[:, None]\n    A = (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    eta = D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    true['confounding'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_exposure(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\n    true['exposure'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, D_true = gen_iv(N_oracle, rng)\n    coef_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_true)\n    coef_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_true)\n    true['iv'] = {\n        'linear_2sls': coef_2sls.tolist(),\n        'control_function': coef_cf.tolist(),\n    }\n    return true\n\n\n# ── MC simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_mc(cms):\n    true_params = compute_true_params()\n    print(\"True parameters (oracle N=2M):\")\n    for topo, vals in true_params.items():\n     "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nExp-B: Logistic DGP Simulation\n\nValidates sign-flip findings under nonlinear (logistic regression) DGP.\n3 topologies (confounding, exposure, IV) × 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)[:, None]\n    A = (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    eta = D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n     "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nExp-B: Logistic DGP Simulation\n\nValidates sign-flip findings under nonlinear (logistic regression) DGP.\n3 topologies (confounding, exposure, IV) × 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)[:, None]\n    A = (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    eta = D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef compute_true_params():\n    N_oracle = 2_000_000\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(9999)\n    true = {}\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_confounding(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    true['confounding'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_exposure(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\n    true['exposure'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, D_true = gen_iv(N_oracle, rng)\n    coef_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_true)\n    coef_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_true)\n    true['iv'] = {\n        'linear_2sls': coef_2sls.tolist(),\n        'control_function': coef_cf.tolist(),\n    }\n    return true\n\n\n# ── MC simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_mc(cms):\n    true_params = compute_true_params()\n    print(\"True parameters (oracle N=2M):\")\n    for topo, vals in true_params.items():\n        for model, coefs in vals.items():\n            print(f\"  {topo}/{model}: {[f'{c:.4f}' for c in coefs]}\")\n\n    results = []\n    n_configs = len(TOPOLOGIES) * len(cms)\n    idx = 0\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        for cm_name, C in cms.items():\n            idx += 1\n            rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + idx * 1000)\n            short_name = cm_name.replace('vast/', '')\n\n            if topo == 'confounding':\n                true_log = np.array(true_params['confounding']['logistic'])\n                true_lin = np.array(true_params['confounding']['linear'])\n            elif topo == 'exposure':\n                true_log = np.array(true_params['exposure']['logistic'])\n                true_lin = np.array(true_params['exposure']['linear'])\n            else:\n                true_2sls = np.array(true_params['iv']['linear_2sls'])\n                true_cf = np.array(true_params['iv']['control_function'])\n\n            log_coefs = []\n            lin_coefs = []\n            log_sf_any = 0\n            lin_sf_any = 0\n            log_sf_tau = 0\n             valid += 1\n                        log_coefs.append(c_log)\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_lin)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)):\n                            log_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin) != np.sign(true_lin)):\n                            lin_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.sign(c_log[0]) != np.sign(true_log[0]):\n                            log_sf_tau += 1\n                        if np.sign(c_lin[0]) != np.sign(true_lin[0]):\n                            lin_sf_tau += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log[1:]) != np.sign(true_log[1:])):\n                            log_sf_dummy += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin[1:]) != log_coefs.append(c_log)\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_lin)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)):\n                            log_sf_any += 1\n            lin_mean_bias = float(np.mean(np.abs(lin_arr - true_2sls[None, :])))\n                    log_coef_means = log_arr.mean(axis=0).tolist()\n                    lin_coef_means =         true_log_list = true_log.tolist()\n                    true_lin_list = true_lin.tolist()\n                    model_log = 'logistic'\n                    model_lin = 'linear'\n            else:\n                log_mean_bias = lin_mean_bias = None\n                log_coef_means = lin_coef_means = None\n                true_log_list = true_lin_list = None\n                model_log = 0 else None\n\n            results.append({\n                'topology': topo,\n                'cm_name': short_name,\n                'model': model_log,\n                'sign_flip_rate': 'n_valid': valid,\n            })\n\n            el = time.time() - t0\n            eta = el / idx * (n_configs - idx) if idx > 0 else 0\n            log_sfr_val = sfr(log_sf_any)\n {eta:.0f}s)\")\n\n    return results, true_params\n\n\ndef build_comparison(results):\n    comp = {}\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        sub = [r for r in results if r['topology'] == topo]\n     "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nExp-B: Logistic DGP Simulation\n\nValidates sign-flip findings under nonlinear (logistic regression) DGP.\n3 topologies (confounding, exposure, IV) × 6 real VAST K=3 CMs\nN=10,000 × 500 MC reps\n\"\"\"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport time\nimport os\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n\nN_SAMPLES = 10_000\nN_REPS = 500\nSEED = 2026\nTOPOLOGIES = ['confounding', 'exposure', 'iv']\n\nTAU = 0.5\nBETA = np.array([0.0, 1.0, -0.5])\nGAMMA_CONF = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\nPI_IV = np.array([0.5, -0.3])\n\nK = 3\nP_A = np.array([1/1, 1/2, 1/3])\nP_A = P_A / P_A.sum()\n\n\ndef load_vast_cms(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    cms = {}\n    for key, val in data.items():\n        if key.startswith('vast/') and val.get('shape', [None])[0] == 3:\n            cm = np.array(val['confusion_matrix_normalized'])\n            if cm.shape == (3, 3):\n                cms[key] = cm\n    names = sorted(cms.keys())[:6]\n    return {n: cms[n] for n in names}\n\n\ndef dummy_encode(A, K=3):\n    N = len(A)\n    D = np.zeros((N, K - 1))\n    for j in range(K - 1):\n        D[:, j] = (A == j + 1).astype(float)\n    return D\n\n\ndef misclassify(A, C, rng):\n    probs = C[:, A]\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=0)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=len(A))\n    return (u[None, :] >= cum[:-1]).sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n\n\n# ── DGP generators ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef gen_confounding(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ GAMMA_CONF + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_exposure(N, rng):\n    T = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    logits = np.column_stack([\n        np.zeros(N),\n        0.3 * T + 0.2,\n        -0.2 * T - 0.1,\n    ])\n    logits -= logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    probs = np.exp(logits)\n    probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    cum = np.cumsum(probs, axis=1)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)[:, None]\n    A = (u >= cum[:, :-1]).sum(axis=1).astype(int)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    eta = D @ BETA[1:] + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, None\n\n\ndef gen_iv(N, rng):\n    A = rng.choice(K, size=N, p=P_A)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    T = D @ PI_IV + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    eta = TAU * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\n    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-eta))\n    Y = rng.binomial(1, prob, N).astype(float)\n    return T, Y, A, D\n\n\nGEN = {'confounding': gen_confounding, 'exposure': gen_exposure, 'iv': gen_iv}\n\n\n# ── Estimators ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    X = np.column_stack([T, D_star])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T, D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(1 + D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    X = D_star\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(X.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(D_star.shape[1], np.nan)\n\n\ndef est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T_hat])\n    try:\n        coef_ss = np.linalg.lstsq(X_ss, Y, rcond=None)[0]\n        return coef_ss[1:]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.array([np.nan])\n\n\ndef est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star):\n    N = len(Y)\n    X_fs = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), D_star])\n    try:\n        coef_fs = np.linalg.lstsq(X_fs, T, rcond=None)[0]\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n    T_hat = X_fs @ coef_fs\n    resid = T - T_hat\n    X_ss = np.column_stack([T, resid])\n    try:\n        lr = LogisticRegression(C=np.inf, max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_ss, Y)\n        return lr.coef_[0]\n    except Exception:\n        return np.full(2, np.nan)\n\n\n# ── True parameters (large-N oracle) ──────────────────────────\n\ndef compute_true_params():\n    N_oracle = 2_000_000\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(9999)\n    true = {}\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_confounding(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D)\n    true['confounding'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, _ = gen_exposure(N_oracle, rng)\n    D = dummy_encode(A)\n    coef_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D)\n    coef_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D)\n    true['exposure'] = {\n        'logistic': coef_log.tolist(),\n        'linear': coef_lin.tolist(),\n    }\n\n    T, Y, A, D_true = gen_iv(N_oracle, rng)\n    coef_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_true)\n    coef_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_true)\n    true['iv'] = {\n        'linear_2sls': coef_2sls.tolist(),\n        'control_function': coef_cf.tolist(),\n    }\n    return true\n\n\n# ── MC simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_mc(cms):\n    true_params = compute_true_params()\n    print(\"True parameters (oracle N=2M):\")\n    for topo, vals in true_params.items():\n        for model, coefs in vals.items():\n            print(f\"  {topo}/{model}: {[f'{c:.4f}' for c in coefs]}\")\n\n    results = []\n    n_configs = len(TOPOLOGIES) * len(cms)\n    idx = 0\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        for cm_name, C in cms.items():\n            idx += 1\n            rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + idx * 1000)\n            short_name = cm_name.replace('vast/', '')\n\n            if topo == 'confounding':\n                true_log = np.array(true_params['confounding']['logistic'])\n                true_lin = np.array(true_params['confounding']['linear'])\n            elif topo == 'exposure':\n                true_log = np.array(true_params['exposure']['logistic'])\n                true_lin = np.array(true_params['exposure']['linear'])\n            else:\n                true_2sls = np.array(true_params['iv']['linear_2sls'])\n                true_cf = np.array(true_params['iv']['control_function'])\n\n            log_coefs = []\n            lin_coefs = []\n            log_sf_any = 0\n            lin_sf_any = 0\n            log_sf_tau = 0\n            lin_sf_tau = 0\n            log_sf_dummy = 0\n            lin_sf_dummy = 0\n            valid = 0\n\n            for m in range(N_REPS):\n                T, Y, A, D_true = GEN[topo](N_SAMPLES, rng)\n                A_star = misclassify(A, C, rng)\n                D_star = dummy_encode(A_star)\n\n                if topo == 'confounding':\n                    c_log = est_logistic_confounding(T, Y, D_star)\n                    c_lin = est_linear_confounding(T, Y, D_star)\n                    if np.all(np.isfinite(c_log)) and np.all(np.isfinite(c_lin)):\n                        valid += 1\n                        log_coefs.append(c_log)\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_lin)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)):\n                            log_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin) != np.sign(true_lin)):\n                            lin_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.sign(c_log[0]) != np.sign(true_log[0]):\n                            log_sf_tau += 1\n                        if np.sign(c_lin[0]) != np.sign(true_lin[0]):\n                            lin_sf_tau += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log[1:]) != np.sign(true_log[1:])):\n                            log_sf_dummy += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin[1:]) != np.sign(true_lin[1:])):\n                            lin_sf_dummy += 1\n\n                elif topo == 'exposure':\n                    c_log = est_logistic_exposure(Y, D_star)\n                    c_lin = est_linear_exposure(Y, D_star)\n                    if np.all(np.isfinite(c_log)) and np.all(np.isfinite(c_lin)):\n                        valid += 1\n                        log_coefs.append(c_log)\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_lin)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)):\n                            log_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_lin) != np.sign(true_lin)):\n                            lin_sf_any += 1\n                        log_sf_dummy += int(np.any(np.sign(c_log) != np.sign(true_log)))\n                        lin_sf_dummy += int(np.any(np.sign(c_lin) != np.sign(true_lin)))\n\n                elif topo == 'iv':\n                    c_2sls = est_linear_2sls(T, Y, D_star)\n                    c_cf = est_control_function_iv(T, Y, D_star)\n                    if np.all(np.isfinite(c_2sls)) and np.all(np.isfinite(c_cf)):\n                        valid += 1\n                        lin_coefs.append(c_2sls)\n                        log_coefs.append(c_cf)\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_2sls) != np.sign(true_2sls)):\n                            lin_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.any(np.sign(c_cf) != np.sign(true_cf)):\n                            log_sf_any += 1\n                        if np.sign(c_2sls[0]) != np.sign(true_2sls[0]):\n                            lin_sf_tau += 1\n                        if np.sign(c_cf[0]) != np.sign(true_cf[0]):\n                            log_sf_tau += 1\n\n            if valid > 0:\n                log_arr = np.array(log_coefs)\n                lin_arr = np.array(lin_coefs)\n\n                if topo == 'iv':\n                    log_mean_bias = float(np.mean(np.abs(log_arr - true_cf[None, :])))\n                    lin_mean_bias = float(np.mean(np.abs(lin_arr - true_2sls[None, :])))\n                    log_coef_means = log_arr.mean(axis=0).tolist()\n                    lin_coef_means = lin_arr.mean(axis=0).tolist()\n                    true_log_list = true_cf.tolist()\n                    true_lin_list = true_2sls.tolist()\n                    model_log = 'control_function'\n                    model_lin = 'linear_2sls'\n                else:\n                    log_mean_bias = float(np.mean(np.abs(log_arr - true_log[None, :])))\n                    lin_mean_bias = float(np.mean(np.abs(lin_arr - true_lin[None, :])))\n                    log_coef_means = log_arr.mean(axis=0).tolist()\n                    lin_coef_means = lin_arr.mean(axis=0).tolist()\n                    true_log_list = true_log.tolist()\n                    true_lin_list = true_lin.tolist()\n                    model_log = 'logistic'\n                    model_lin = 'linear'\n            else:\n                log_mean_bias = lin_mean_bias = None\n                log_coef_means = lin_coef_means = None\n                true_log_list = true_lin_list = None\n                model_log = 'logistic' if topo != 'iv' else 'control_function'\n                model_lin = 'linear' if topo != 'iv' else 'linear_2sls'\n\n            def sfr(cnt):\n                return cnt / valid if valid > 0 else None\n\n            results.append({\n                'topology': topo,\n                'cm_name': short_name,\n                'model': model_log,\n                'sign_flip_rate': sfr(log_sf_any),\n                'tau_sign_flip_rate': sfr(log_sf_tau),\n                'dummy_sign_flip_rate': sfr(log_sf_dummy),\n                'mean_bias': log_mean_bias,\n                'coef_means': log_coef_means,\n                'true_coefs': true_log_list,\n                'n_valid': valid,\n            })\n            results.append({\n                'topology': topo,\n                'cm_name': short_name,\n                'model': model_lin,\n                'sign_flip_rate': sfr(lin_sf_any),\n                'tau_sign_flip_rate': sfr(lin_sf_tau),\n                'dummy_sign_flip_rate': sfr(lin_sf_dummy),\n                'mean_bias': lin_mean_bias,\n                'coef_means': lin_coef_means,\n                'true_coefs': true_lin_list,\n                'n_valid': valid,\n            })\n\n            el = time.time() - t0\n            eta = el / idx * (n_configs - idx) if idx > 0 else 0\n            log_sfr_val = sfr(log_sf_any)\n            lin_sfr_val = sfr(lin_sf_any)\n            log_s = f\"{log_sfr_val:.3f}\" if log_sfr_val is not None else \"N/A\"\n            lin_s = f\"{lin_sfr_val:.3f}\" if lin_sfr_val is not None else \"N/A\"\n            print(f\"  [{idx}/{n_configs}] {topo:<13} {short_name:<30} \"\n                  f\"log_SF={log_s} lin_SF={lin_s} \"\n                  f\"valid={valid}/{N_REPS} ({el:.0f}s, ETA {eta:.0f}s)\")\n\n    return results, true_params\n\n\ndef build_comparison(results):\n    comp = {}\n    for topo in TOPOLOGIES:\n        sub = [r for r in results if r['topology'] == topo]\n        if topo == 'iv':\n            log_model = 'control_function'\n            lin_model = 'linear_2sls'\n        else:\n            log_model = 'logistic'\n            lin_model = 'linear'\n\n        def extract(model, field):\n            return [r[field] for r in sub\n                    if r['model'] == model and r[field] is not None]\n\n        comp[topo] = {\n            'nonlinear_mean_sf': float(np.mean(extract(log_model, 'sign_flip_rate'))) if extract(log_model, 'sign_flip_rate') else None,\n            'linear_mean_sf': float(np.mean(extract(lin_model, 'sign_flip_rate'))) if extract(lin_model, 'sign_flip_rate') else None,\n            'nonlinear_mean_tau_sf': float(np.mean(extract(log_model, 'tau_sign_flip_rate'))) if extract(log_model, 'tau_sign_flip_rate') else None,\n            'linear_mean_tau_sf': float(np.mean(extract(lin_model, 'tau_sign_flip_rate'))) if extract(lin_model, 'tau_sign_flip_rate') else None,\n            'nonlinear_model': log_model,\n            'linear_model': lin_model,\n            'n_cms': len(extract(log_model, 'sign_flip_rate')),\n        }\n    return comp\n\n\ndef write_tex(results, path):\n    lines = [\n        r'\\begin{table}[t]',\n        r'\\centering',\n        r'\\caption{Sign flip rates under linear vs.\\ logistic DGP across 6 real confusion matrices (VAST, $K\\!=\\!3$). '\n        r'SF$_\\tau$: treatment coefficient only; SF$_\\text{any}$: any coefficient. '\n        r'Each cell: $N\\!=\\!10{,}000 \\times 500$ MC reps.}',\n        r'\\label{tab:logistic-robustness}',\n        r'\\small\\setlength{\\tabcolsep}{3pt}',\n r'Topology & CM & SF$_\\tau$ & SF$_\\text{any}$ & SF$_\\tau$ & SF$_\\text{any}$ \\\\',\n        r'\\midrule',\n    ]\n\n    for ti, topo in enumerate(TOPOLOGIES):\n        if ti > 0:\n            lines.append(r'\\midrule')\n        topo_label = {'confounding': 'Confounding', 'exposure': 'Exposure', 'iv': 'IV'}[topo]\n        if topo == 'iv':\n            lin_model = 'linear_2sls'\n            log_model = 'control_function'\n        else:\n            lin_model = 'linear'\n            log_model = 'logistic'\n\n        sub = [r for r in results if r['topology'] == topo]\n        cm_names = sorted(set(r['cm_name'] for r in sub))\n        first = True\n        for cm in cm_names:\n            lin_r = next((r for r in sub if r['cm_name'] == cm and r['model'] == lin_model), None)\n            log_r = next((r for r in sub if r['cm_name'] == cm and r['model'] == log_model), None)\n\n            def fmt(r, field):\n                if r and r.get(field) is not None:\n                    return f\"{r[field]:.3f}\"\n                return '--'\n\n            tl = topo_label if first else ''\n            cm_short = cm.replace('_', r'\\_')\n            if topo == 'exposure':\n                lin_tau = '--'\n                log_tau = '--'\n            else:\n                lin_tau = fmt(lin_r, 'tau_sign_flip_rate')\n                log_tau = fmt(log_r, 'tau_sign_flip_rate')\n            lin_any = fmt(lin_r, 'sign_flip_rate')\n            log_any = fmt(log_r, 'sign_flip_rate')\n            lines.append(f'{tl} & {cm_short} & {lin_tau} & {lin_any} & {log_tau} & {log_any} \\\\\\\\')\n            first = False\n\n    lines += [\n        r'\\bottomrule',\n        r'\\end{tabular}',\n        r'\\end{table}',\n    ]\n    with open(path, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('\\n'.join(lines) + '\\n')\n\n\ndef clean(o):\n    if isinstance(o, (float, np.floating)):\n        v = float(o)\n        return None if (np.isnan(v) or np.isinf(v)) else v\n    if isinstance(o, np.integer):\n        return int(o)\n    if isinstance(o, dict):\n        return {k: clean(v) for k, v in o.items()}\n    if isinstance(o, list):\n        return [clean(v) for v in o]\n    return o\n\n\ndef main():\n    t0 = time.time()\n    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n    cm_path = os.path.join(out_dir, 'plan011_frontier_cms.json')\n\n    cms = load_vast_cms(cm_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(cms)} VAST K=3 CMs: {list(cms.keys())}\")\n\n    results, true_params = run_mc(cms)\n\n    comp = build_comparison(results)\n    output = clean({\n        'results': results,\n        'comparison_with_linear': comp,\n        'true_params': true_params,\n        'config': {\n            'N': N_SAMPLES, 'n_reps': N_REPS, 'K': K,\n            'tau': TAU, 'beta': BETA.tolist(),\n            'topologies': TOPOLOGIES,\n        },\n    })\n\n    jpath = os.path.join(out_dir, 'exp_b_logistic_results.json')\n    with open(jpath, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nJSON -> {jpath}\")\n\n    tpath = os.path.join(out_dir, 'exp_b_logistic_table.tex')\n    write_tex(results, tpath)\n    print(f\"TeX  -> {tpath}\")\n\n    total = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"\\nDone in {total:.0f}s ({total/60:.1f}min)\")\n\n    print(\"\\nComparison summary:\")\n    for topo, c in comp.items():\n        nl = c['nonlinear_mean_sf']\n        li = c['linear_mean_sf']\n        nls = f\"{nl:.4f}\" if nl is not None else 'N/A'\n        lis = f\"{li:.4f}\" if li is not None else 'N/A'\n        print(f\"  {topo:<13}: linear={lis} nonlinear={nls} ({c['nonlinear_model']})\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n",
